## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner for work on this machine. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repositories.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 이 컴퓨터에서 어떤 저장소의 실패한 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하거나, 메일·일정을 확인해 달라고 하면 바로 도와드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性のある順序どおりの配送」を保証し、UDPは保証せず速さと軽さを優先する、という点が一番の違いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama local al día con la principal antes de abrir el PR, sin ensuciar el historial. Usa merge para integrar algo que ya compartiste con otros o cuando quieres conservar exactamente cómo fue el trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据有序、不丢不重地送达；UDP 不保证这些，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to keep history linear and tidy before it merges into main. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rewriting published history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 我去把 compaction 那个 PR 的锁粒度细化，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话：「接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点」

要做的：
- 按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把 compaction 路径上的锁改细，缩小临界区，避免无关操作互相阻塞。
- 改完跑相关测试，确认行为没变，并在报告里附上测试结果。
- 改动提交到这个任务自己的分支上，PR 保持 draft。

不在范围内：
- 不合并到主分支，不推送到远端之外的地方，不把 draft 改成 ready，不在 PR 上发评论或回复 review。这些等用户说 Go 再做。

完成标准：

- 如果有 review 里的锁问题没能处理，直接说明原因。

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没查</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>

  </div>

</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var LIMIT = 600;
  function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
    return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]; }); }
  function render(d) {
    if (!d || !d.trains) return;
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var under = d.trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price <= LIMIT; });
    var sorted = d.trains.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    if (under.length) {
      var min = Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function (t) { return t.price; }));
      document.getElementById('sub').textContent = '最低 ¥' + min + ' · ' +
        under.map(function (t) { return t.no; }).join('、');
    } else {
      document.getElementById('sub').textContent = '暂时没有 600 元以下的班次';
    }
    list.innerHTML = sorted.map(function (t) {
      var ok = t.price <= LIMIT;
      return '<div class="k-row">' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) + '　' + esc(t.seats) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value" style="color:' + (ok ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)') + '">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');
    document.getElementById('upd').textContent = (d.date ? d.date + ' · ' : '') + (d.updated ? '更新于 ' + d.updated : '');
  }
  var v = ducter.get('fares');
  if (v && v.then) v.then(render); else render(v);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    var n = ducter.get('fares');
    if (n && n.then) n.then(render); else render(n);
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":{"date":"周五 11/28 · 北京南→上海虹桥","updated":"11-24 早 8:00","trains":[{"no":"G1","seats":"二等座余 12 张","price":553},{"no":"G3","seats":"二等座余 4 张","price":553},{"no":"G7","seats":"二等座","price":626}]}}

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the nightly write benchmark board is up with nights 2 and 3 filled in (182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, 0 stalls on night 3). It will add a row each night as the benchmark reports.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1849 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Writes per second, p99 latency</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <script>
    function fmt(n) { return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + "k" : String(n); }
    function render(nights) {
      var list = document.getElementById("list");
      list.innerHTML = "";
      if (!nights || !nights.length) {
        var empty = document.createElement("div");
        empty.className = "k-empty";
        empty.textContent = "Each night's write speed, p99 and compaction stalls will list here.";
        list.appendChild(empty);
        return;
      }
      var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
      document.getElementById("latest").textContent = fmt(last.writes) + " writes/s";
      document.getElementById("latest-sub").textContent =
        "p99 " + last.p99 + " ms · compaction stalls " + (last.stalls == null ? "not recorded" : last.stalls);
      nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
        var row = document.createElement("div");
        row.className = "k-row";
        row.innerHTML =
          '<div class="k-row-main">Night ' + n.night + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + (n.stalls == null ? "not recorded" : n.stalls) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(n.writes) + '/s</div>';
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("nights")).then(render);
    ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get("nights")).then(render); });
    ducter.summary("Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms");
  </script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":null},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("en-US");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  const tot=document.getElementById("total");
  const sub=document.getElementById("sub");
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  if(xs.length){tot.textContent=fmt(total);}
  else{tot.innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';}
  sub.textContent=xs.length?("共 "+xs.length+" 笔"):"记下第一笔后这里会显示合计";
  list.innerHTML="";
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销";list.appendChild(e);return;}
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sb=document.createElement("div");sb.className="k-row-sub";sb.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan)||0);
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(sb);row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  if(typeof ducter.summary==="function"){ducter.summary(xs.length?(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total)):"还没记");}
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: "PRs waiting on others" is on your shelf with the four PRs. Tap one when it lands and it gets ticked off, and the count at the top updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2326 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tap a PR when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-h">PRs waiting on someone</div>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<style>
.pr-row{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;width:100%;background:none;border:0;text-align:left;color:var(--d-ink);padding:10px 0;cursor:pointer;font:inherit}
.pr-tick{width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;border:1.5px solid var(--d-line);display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin-right:10px;flex:none;color:var(--d-good);font-weight:700;font-size:14px}
.pr-done .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.pr-done .pr-tick{background:var(--d-good);border-color:var(--d-good);color:var(--d-card)}
.pr-sub{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px;margin-top:2px}
</style>
<script>
var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
var done = ducter.get('done') || {};

function render() {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.textContent = '';
  var landed = 0;
  PRS.forEach(function (p) {
    var isDone = !!done[p.id];
    if (isDone) landed++;
    var row = document.createElement('button');
    row.className = 'pr-row' + (isDone ? ' pr-done' : '');
    var tick = document.createElement('span');
    tick.className = 'pr-tick';
    tick.textContent = isDone ? '✓' : '';
    var text = document.createElement('span');
    var t = document.createElement('div');
    t.className = 'k-row-main';
    t.textContent = p.title;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'pr-sub';
    s.textContent = p.note;
    text.appendChild(t);
    text.appendChild(s);
    row.appendChild(tick);
    row.appendChild(text);
    row.onclick = function () {
      done[p.id] = !done[p.id];
      ducter.set('done', done);
      render();
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var open = PRS.length - landed;
  document.getElementById('open').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = landed ? (landed + ' landed') : 'tap a PR when it lands';
  ducter.summary(open + ' waiting · ' + landed + ' landed');
}

ducter.onChange(function () {
  done = ducter.get('done') || {};
  render();
});
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter#412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","note":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem#10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","note":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb#1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","note":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter#418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","note":"Draft, not requested yet"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(Number(x.amt||0));
    r.append(m,s,v);list.appendChild(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total));
}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!$("amt").value||!(amt>0))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const item={trip:$("trip").value.trim(),cat:cat,day:$("day").value.trim(),amt:amt};
  if(editing>=0){if(xs[editing]&&xs[editing].from)item.from=xs[editing].from;xs[editing]=item}else xs.push(item);
  ducter.set("items",xs);reset();$("add").open=false;draw();
};
$("cancel").onclick=()=>{reset();$("add").open=false};
$("del").onclick=()=>{
  if(editing<0)return;
  const b=$("del");
  if(b.dataset.armed!=="1"){b.dataset.armed="1";b.textContent="再点一次确认删除";return}
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];xs.splice(editing,1);ducter.set("items",xs);
  b.dataset.armed="0";reset();$("add").open=false;draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
buildCats();reset();draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","cat":"交通","day":"11/20","amt":662,"from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","cat":"住宿","day":"11/20–21","amt":1036,"from":"你的预订"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

要求：
- 在现有的假时钟 TTL 测试基础上，补一条测试，覆盖时间正好等于过期时刻的情况。
- 需要明确断言：在恰好到期的那一刻，值是否已过期，并与实现的实际语义保持一致（不要为了通过测试而改实现）。
- 同时检查相邻的一刻（到期前一瞬、到期后一瞬），确保边界行为确定、不抖动。
- 测试必须使用假时钟，不能依赖真实计时。
- 完成后把新测试跑 20 次，全部通过，并把结果附在报告里。

范围外：不要合并或推送到主分支，不要改动其他无关测试。提交在当前分支 ducter/foo-ttl 上即可。
```
